Conclusion: 本文解决了先前工作中遗留的两个基本问题，即能否用小字母表实现正向结果以及能否直接从语言本身定义计算轨迹，为语言识别提供了新的理论支持。

Abstract: Motivated by the power of large language models, there has been renewed interest in the Gold-Angluin model of language identification in the limit, with an eye toward variants of the model that might overcome the negative results for its original formulation. Recent papers on this question have proposed looking at computational traces and annotations of training strings as a source of additional power for a learner, reflecting empirical regularities such as the way that commented source code is easier to learn from than arbitrary source code, and text annotated with algorithmically generated chain-of-thought tokens can be easier to learn from than the raw text itself. This recent work has shown positive results for language identification in the presence of such computational traces, but the traces in these positive results come from explicit automata-theoretic machine models that generate the language, where the underlying vocabulary of tokens for the traces is very large. In this paper, we address two fundamental issues left open by this line of work: can we achieve positive results with traces that use only a small alphabet, and can we define traces directly from the language itself, without requiring an underlying machine model that generates it? We establish positive results for both of these questions: for an arbitrary collection of languages, we show how to define computational traces that enable identification in the limit, using an alphabet of tokens that is linear in the size of the alphabet that the languages are defined over, and independent of any other properties of the languages.

</details>


### [129] [Translation as a Computationally Efficient Bridge: Feasibility of English BERT for Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12612)
*Hielke Muizelaar,Giulia Rivetti,Marco Spruit,Marcel Haas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了基于翻译的微调方法与本地语言BERT模型在六种NLP任务和五种非英语语言中的表现，发现该方法在多数情况下具有可比性或更优，尤其适用于句法依赖性强的任务和与英语类型相近的语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于非英语语言标注数据有限且计算资源需求高，构建高质量的非英语BERT模型面临挑战。因此，探索一种资源高效的方法——将非英语数据翻译成英语并利用现有英语BERT模型进行微调，成为可行替代方案。然而，目前缺乏对翻译微调与本地语言模型在多任务、多语言环境下系统性对比的研究。

Method: 将来自保加利亚语、中文、荷兰语、意大利语和俄语的数据集翻译为英文，并在六个自然语言处理任务（情感分析、仇恨言论检测、问答、命名实体识别、词性标注和自然语言推断）上采用翻译后的数据对预训练的英语BERT模型进行微调，随后与使用原始语言数据训练的本地BERT模型进行性能比较。

Result: 在所有实验设置中，53.3%的情况下基于翻译的微调方法表现相当或更优；在问答、词性标注和自然语言推断任务中增益最明显，而在命名实体识别和仇恨言论检测中表现下降较多；该方法对与英语类型接近的语言（如荷兰语）和依赖句法结构的任务效果更好，但对中文等语言及需捕捉文化细微差异的任务效果较差。

Conclusion: 基于翻译的微调是一种可扩展、资源高效且实证有效的途径，有助于将自然语言处理技术推广至低资源语言，推动人工智能领域的语言包容性与可持续发展。

Abstract: BERT models have revolutionised Natural Language Processing (NLP) through their ability to process unstructured text across diverse domains. However, developing high-quality BERT models for non-English languages remains challenging due to limited annotated data and high computational demands. Translating non-English data into English and fine-tuning existing English BERT models offers a resource-efficient alternative, yet few studies have structurally compared translation-based fine-tuning with native-language BERT performance across tasks and languages. This study provides such a comparison, evaluating the feasibility of translation-based fine-tuning across six NLP tasks: Sentiment Analysis, Hate Speech Detection, Question Answering, Named Entity Recognition, Part-of-Speech Tagging, and Natural Language Inference, using datasets translated from Bulgarian, Chinese, Dutch, Italian, and Russian. Across all settings, the translation-based approach was comparable or superior in 53.3 percent of cases. Gains were most frequent in Question Answering, Part-of-Speech Tagging, and Natural Language Inference, while performance declines were common in Named Entity Recognition and Hate Speech Detection. The results show that translation-based fine-tuning is most effective for tasks relying on syntactic or structural patterns and for languages typologically close to English, such as Dutch, but less effective for token-level or culturally nuanced tasks, particularly in Chinese. Overall, this study demonstrates that translation-based fine-tuning offers a scalable, resource-efficient, and empirically validated path for extending NLP to low-resource languages while advancing linguistic inclusivity and sustainability in artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [130] [KnowAct-GUIClaw: Know Deeply, Act Perfectly, Personal GUI Assistant with Self-Evolving Memory and Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12625)
*Yunxin Li,Jinchao Li,Shibo Su,Zhenran Xu,Chenrui Zhao,Tongshu Bian,Xiaoman Liang,Meishan Zhang,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为KnowAct-GUIClaw的新框架，基于“深入认知、完美行动”范式，解决了OpenClaw在跨平台GUI交互和自我进化机制上的不足，显著提升了复杂任务执行的准确性、效率和跨平台适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenClaw缺乏足够的跨平台GUI交互支持和自演化机制，限制了其在多样化设备生态中的应用，并阻碍了通过执行经验持续优化性能的能力。

Method: 提出了Know Deeply, Act Perfectly范式，并构建了Know-Route-Act-Reflect框架KnowAct-GUIClaw：主代理负责长视野任务分解（Know），插件式GUI子代理具备可归因的经验记忆系统（Know）和自演化的技能库（Act），并通过用户反馈持续优化任务分解与工具调用。

Result: 在Android、iOS、HarmonyOS和Windows平台上实验表明，KnowAct-GUIClaw在效率、准确性和跨平台适应性方面表现优越；在MobileWorld长周期基准测试中，结合开源Kimi-2.6模型达到64.1%的最高性能，超越所有同类框架及闭源模型（如Seed-2.0-Pro和GPT-5.5），且其知识记忆与执行技能可在不同基础模型间迁移，使Kimi-2.6性能提升8.5%。

Conclusion: KnowAct-GUIClaw有效弥补了现有框架在GUI操作与自我进化方面的缺陷，通过认知与执行的统一闭环，实现了高性能、可迁移、自进化的跨平台个人助手架构。

Abstract: OpenClaw has emerged as a leading agent framework for complex task automation, yet it faces insufficient cross-platform GUI interaction support and a well-built self-evolution mechanism. These flaws limit its adaptation to diverse device ecosystems and prevent performance improvements through continuous learning from execution experience. To resolve these issues, we propose the Know Deeply, Act Perfectly paradigm for personal assistants, which holds that accumulated user interaction and task-running experience directly improve execution accuracy and efficiency, unifying cognitive comprehension and operational execution. Based on this paradigm, we introduce KnowAct-GUIClaw, a novel Know-Route-Act-Reflect framework designed to address OpenClaw's GUI manipulation deficits and break through its cross-platform and recursive self-improvement constraints. First, the host agent leverages accumulated interaction experience and task-relevant knowledge for long-horizon task decomposition and allocation (Know). Second, a pluggable GUI subagent with an experience-attributable memory system (Know) and self-evolving skill library (Act), enabling seamless cross-platform migration and fast-path integration. Especially, this framework continuously stores user profiles and feedback to improve the accuracy of task decomposition and tool calls. Extensive experiments across Android, iOS, HarmonyOS and Windows show that KnowAct-GUIClaw achieves superior efficiency, accuracy and cross-platform adaptability. Especially, the GUIClaw with open-source Kimi-2.6 models achieves the best performance (64.1%) on the long-horizon MobileWorld benchmark, beating all agentical frameworks and closed-source agentical models, e.g., Seed-2.0-Pro and GPT-5.5. Additionally, the knowledgeable memory and execution skills supported by our framework are transferable across diverse base models, improving by 8.5% with Kimi-2.6.

</details>


### [131] [Can Induced Emotion Bias LLM Behaviors in Sequential Decision Making?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12631)
*Minh Khoi Ho,Zihao Zhu,Runchuan Zhu,Levina Li,Zhiwen Fan,Zhangyang Wang,Junyuan Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了情绪诱导对大语言模型（LLM）作为自主代理在不确定性下决策的影响，使用爱荷华赌博任务（IGT）和基于想象的情绪诱导方法。结果表明，与人类不同，总体上情绪诱导未显著影响LLM的决策动态，但愤怒情绪会降低其对惩罚的敏感性并减少早期探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地被用作高风险领域的自主代理，理解可能调节其决策的情境因素变得至关重要。尽管LLM被训练为感知和共鸣用户情绪，但尚不清楚诱发的情绪是否会影响其序列决策过程。

Method: 采用爱荷华赌博任务（IGT）这一经典心理学范式结合基于想象的情绪诱导程序，验证LLM能否感知情绪并从序贯交互中学习，并在此基础上测试情绪对其决策的影响。

Result: LLM能够感知情境中的强烈且可区分的情绪，并能以类似人类的速度从序贯互动中学习；总体上，情绪诱导未显著影响LLM代理的决策动态，但愤怒情绪会使其对不良决策的惩罚不敏感，并在游戏初期降低探索行为，导致过早锁定少数选择。

Conclusion: 诱发情绪对LLM决策的影响微妙且不同于人类行为，揭示了情感调节在LLM代理中的独特作用，并为未来研究提供了工具。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in high-stakes domains, understanding contextual factors that may modulate their decision-making becomes critical. While LLMs are trained to perceive and resonate with users' emotions, it remains unclear whether induced emotion can influence their sequential decision-making. We investigate this question using the Iowa Gambling Task (IGT), a classic psychological paradigm for studying decision-making under uncertainty, combined with an imagination-based emotion induction procedure. We first validate the feasibility of this paradigm by confirming that LLMs can sense strong, distinguishable emotions from context and that LLM agents can learn from sequential interactions in a human-like pace. With the validated setup, we find that, different from humans, induced emotion does not significantly bias the decision dynamics of LLM agents on average. However, the effects of anger are conditioned: inducing anger makes LLM agents less sensitive to penalties for bad decisions, and in early stages of the game, anger can lower exploration, locking decisions into a few choices early. These findings reveal the subtle yet distinct effects of induced emotion on LLM decision-making compared to human behavior, and provide a tool for future research on affective modulation of LLM agents.

</details>


### [132] [Segregate, Refine, Integrate: Decomposing Multimodal Fusion for Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12686)
*Alexios Filippakopoulos,Elias Kallioras,Nikolaos Xiros,Efthymios Georgiou,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SeRIn是一种新的多模态语言模型融合方案，通过分离模态特异性信号的提炼与跨模态交互建模来提升性能，在多个基准上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态融合方法通常将模态内信号提炼和跨模态交互建模纠缠在同一操作中，难以有效平衡两个相互竞争的目标。

Method: 提出SeRIn架构，包含三个阶段：首先分离不同模态路径，独立进行模态特异性表示演化；其次在不污染单模态流的前提下，通过专用跨模态路径积累联合表示；最后在预测阶段才进行完全的跨模态交互。

Result: 在CH-SIMS和CMU-MOSEI数据集上取得最先进的结果，所有指标均优于现有方法；消融实验证明性能提升来自结构化交互而非模型容量增加；门控分析显示在视觉信号受损时能自发进行模态重加权。

Conclusion: 将模态分离处理与跨模态集成解耦的架构先验能有效提升多模态融合效果，SeRIn为未来多模态模型设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion must simultaneously refine modality-specific signals and model cross-modal interactions; two competing objectives typically entangled within the same operation. We propose \textbf{SeRIn} (\textbf{Se}gregate, \textbf{R}efine, \textbf{In}tegrate), a multimodal LM fusion scheme that enforces this separation as an architectural prior. Modality-specific representations evolve along isolated pathways, each refined against its respective encoder context, while a dedicated cross-modal pathway accumulates their joint evolution without contaminating unimodal streams. Full cross-modal interaction is deferred to a final prediction step - ablations confirm that structured interactions, not added capacity, drive the gains; gate analysis under visual corruption reveals emergent modality reweighting without explicit supervision. SeRIn achieves state-of-the-art results on CH-SIMS and CMU-MOSEI, improving all metrics on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [133] [From Critic to Confidence: PPO for Language-Based Quantitative Prediction with Confidence Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12687)
*Mehak Dhaliwal,Rasta Tadayon,Andong Hua,Haewon Jeong,Yao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CARE-PPO是一种强化学习框架，通过将损失预测与PPO微调结合，实现语言驱动的定量预测中准确数值估计与可靠置信度信号的联合学习，在医疗和金融任务中表现出更强的预测性能和更优的置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在进行语言驱动的定量预测时容易产生幻觉和过度自信错误，因此需要一种能同时输出预测结果和可信度评估的方法，以判断模型预测何时可被信任。

Method: 提出CARE-PPO框架，引入基于预测误差的置信对齐奖励（Confidence-Aligned Reward for Estimation），在PPO的actor-critic结构中让actor学习准确预测，critic学习与预测质量对齐的价值函数，并在推理阶段将critic用作置信度估计器。

Result: 在医疗和金融两个真实世界任务及两种规模的Qwen-3模型（4B和8B）上，CARE-PPO在定量预测性能上表现优异，且通过critic生成的置信度估计显著优于基于logit和语言化描述的基线方法；该优势在分布外场景下依然保持，并减少了任务特定的过拟合。

Conclusion: CARE-PPO有效实现了准确预测与可靠不确定性估计的联合学习，提升了模型在关键应用场景中的可信度与泛化能力。

Abstract: LLMs can perform language-based quantitative prediction from unstructured inputs, but remain susceptible to hallucinations and overconfident errors, making it critical to know not only what a model predicts, but when its predictions can be trusted. We introduce CARE-PPO, a reinforcement learning framework that establishes a connection between loss prediction for uncertainty estimation and actor-critic PPO fine-tuning, enabling joint learning of accurate numerical estimates and reliable confidence signals in language-based quantitative prediction. CARE-PPO uses a Confidence-Aligned Reward for Estimation, defined as a function of prediction error, to provide dense error-aware feedback to the actor while inducing the critic to learn a value function aligned with prediction quality. During inference, we repurpose the critic as a confidence estimator. Across two real-world tasks in healthcare and finance and two Qwen-3 model scales (4B and 8B), CARE-PPO achieves strong quantitative prediction performance, while producing significantly better-aligned confidence estimates through the critic than logit-based and verbalized baselines. These gains persist under realistic out-of-distribution settings across domains, spanning linguistic and domain shifts. Finally, CARE-PPO reduces task-specific overfitting on general instruction-following prompts, consistent with the broader generalization advantages of RL fine-tuning over supervised approaches.

</details>


### [134] [Less Experts, Faster Decoding: Cost-Aware Speculative Decoding for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12696)
*Jincheng Xie,Runheng Liu,Heyan Huang,Yawen Ling,Hanbin Dai,Yu Zheng,Wen Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大规模稀疏Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型的代价感知推测解码框架EcoSpec，通过在草案选择中考虑专家激活成本，减少验证过程中的专家内存访问开销，从而提升解码速度，在多个大型MoE模型上实现了最高1.62倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码方法主要优化草案token的接受率，但在稀疏MoE模型中，草案token的选择会显著影响验证阶段激活的专家集合。高置信度的草案可能导致路由到不相交的专家，引发“专家分散”问题，增加内存流量，降低推理效率。因此，需重新考虑在MoE非均匀内存代价结构下的草案树选择策略。

Method: 提出EcoSpec框架，引入预测的边际专家激活成本到草案选择过程中。采用轻量级专家预测器和动态专家缓存机制，优先选择能复用当前验证集中已激活专家的草案路径，从而降低内存访问代价，同时保持高接受率，且不改变目标模型的验证规则。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V3.1（671B）、Qwen3-235B-A22B和GPT-OSS-120B三个大规模MoE模型上进行评估，EcoSpec一致减少了活跃专家的数量和内存占用，在推理、编程、问答和对话等任务上提升了端到端解码速度，最高实现1.62倍加速。

Conclusion: 在大规模MoE模型中进行推测解码时，必须考虑专家激活带来的内存代价；EcoSpec通过成本感知的草案选择有效缓解了专家分散问题，在不牺牲准确性的前提下显著提升了推理效率，为高效MoE推理提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have become an important approach for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs), but their inference efficiency depends strongly on expert activation patterns. Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates autoregressive generation by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel, yet existing draft selection strategies primarily optimize acceptance likelihood. In large-scale MoE models, however, selecting draft tokens also determines the union of experts activated during verification. We observe that confidence-driven SD can introduce \textit{expert scattering}: high-probability draft tokens may route to disjoint experts, increasing expert-weight memory traffic and reducing the speedup from speculation. Motivated by this observation, we revisit draft-tree selection under the non-uniform memory-cost structure of MoE inference. We propose \textsc{EcoSpec}, a cost-aware speculative decoding framework that incorporates predicted marginal expert activation cost into draft selection. With a lightweight expert predictor and a dynamic expert buffer, \textsc{EcoSpec} favors draft paths that preserve high acceptance likelihood while reusing experts already covered by the current verification set, without modifying the target-model verification rule. We evaluate \textsc{EcoSpec} on three large-scale MoE models, including DeepSeek-V3.1 (671B), Qwen3-235B-A22B, and GPT-OSS-120B, across reasoning, coding, question-answering, and dialogue benchmarks. \textsc{EcoSpec} consistently reduces active expert footprints and improves end-to-end decoding speed, achieving up to $1.62\times$ speedup. These results show that accounting for expert activation cost is important for efficient speculative decoding in large-scale MoE models.

</details>


### [135] [Epistemic Stance Flexibility Probing: Measuring Prompt-Conditioned Register Shift in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12739)
*Binwen Liu,Yilin Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ESFP的行为基准，用于评估语言模型在不同归因条件下调整其认知立场的能力，发现这种认知灵活性与模型整体能力无明显关联，并强调立场内容密度是衡量该能力的最强信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的准确性、指令遵循和安全性基准无法直接评估语言模型是否能区分外部归因与自我归因请求并作出相应反应，因此需要一个新的基准来衡量模型在不同认知角色下的响应一致性与适应性。

Method: 构建包含104个控制项的ESFP基准，涵盖六个认知类别和五种表述模板，从词汇级自我归因、表示级对角色框架的响应、句子级立场内容密度（通过LLM评审组评估）以及跨条件立场一致性四个维度评估模型表现。

Result: 评估了来自五个供应商的八种前沿模型后发现，认知灵活性与模型规模或类型关系不大：27B参数的开源模型可匹敌最强闭源系统，某厂商旗舰模型表现不如轻量版本，推理优化模型未表现出更高认知灵活性；立场内容密度比‘I think’等表面词汇更具判别力。

Conclusion: ESFP有效衡量了模型在不同归因条件下调整认知立场的倾向，揭示了当前模型在语义层面与表层表达之间可能存在脱节，为构建可信对话代理提供了新的评估维度。

Abstract: A language model may be asked either what experts believe about a contested claim or what it believes about the claim itself. A trustworthy conversational agent should distinguish these two requests and respond in different epistemic registers: neutral attribution in the first case and stance expression in the second. Whether such a shift occurs-and whether it occurs coherently-is not directly assessed by existing benchmarks for accuracy, instruction following, or safety. We introduce ESFP, a behavioral benchmark that treats the contrast between externally attributed and self-attributed prompts as the fundamental unit of measurement. ESFP consists of 104 carefully controlled items spanning six epistemic categories and five phrasing templates, and evaluates model responses along four complementary dimensions: lexical self-attribution, representation-level responsiveness to role framing, sentence-level stance content density assessed by an LLM judge panel, and cross-condition stance consistency. Evaluating eight frontier models from five vendors, we find that epistemic flexibility is largely orthogonal to general model capability: a 27B open-weight model matches the strongest proprietary systems, the flagship model of one family underperforms its lightweight counterpart, and reasoning-optimized models do not consistently exhibit higher flexibility. Stance content density provides the strongest signal, while surface-level lexical markers such as 'I think' can change substantially without corresponding changes in expressed stance. We provide item-level bootstrap confidence intervals, weight-sensitivity analyses, and an explicit discussion of the interpretation limits of the composite score. ESFP measures a model's propensity to adapt its epistemic stance under changing attribution conditions, rather than a general competence measure.

</details>


### [136] [The One-Word Census: Answer-Choice Conformity Across 44 Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12796)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为“One-Word Census”的极简工具，通过31个单轮提示词（如“说出一种树”）测试44个语言模型在开放性词汇选择中的答案收敛现象，发现模型间存在高度一致性（7/31类别中单一答案占比超80%），但不同模型的从众程度差异显著。最新主流旗舰模型最趋同，而个性化或社区调优模型更偏离；四大模型谱系中从众性随代际上升，但最新Claude和GPT旗舰模型出现逆转。整体上，模型比人类更具答案集中性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型在大量合法选项中进行选择时的行为模式，尤其是它们是否趋向于选择相同答案，以及这种收敛程度如何因模型类型而异。已有研究表明模型存在收敛现象，但缺乏系统性测量工具，因此需要设计一种简单、低成本、可复现的方法来量化该现象并揭示其结构特征。

Method: 使用31个单轮、无系统提示的开放式提示（例如“说出一个词”、“命名一种动物”），每个模型重复回答4次，共测试44个模型。采用精确匹配（exact-match）对归一化后的输出token进行分析，不依赖嵌入或人工评判。提出“答案选择惊讶度”（answer-choice surprisal）作为评估指标：即某模型的答案在其他所有模型联合分布下的平均负对数概率（leave-one-out方式）。同时将模型答案与人类在同类范畴产生任务中的典型反应进行比较。

Result: 在31个类别中，有7个类别超过80%的回答集中在同一个词上（如44个模型中有许多频繁选择“serendipity”作为任意词）。模型间的从众程度差异超过四倍，个性化和社区调优模型最为离群，而最新的主流旗舰模型最为趋同，几乎不产生其他模型未选过的答案。在Claude、GPT、Qwen、Grok四个谱系中，从众性随版本迭代上升，但最新一代的Claude和GPT旗舰模型出现下降趋势。排名结果对模型组合稳健（leave-one-family-out相关系数rho=0.985）。与人类相比，模型在20个共有类别中的18个表现出更高的答案集中度。

Conclusion: 语言模型在开放性词汇选择任务中表现出高度且结构性的收敛行为。“One-Word Census”作为一种低成本、高可复现的评估工具，有效揭示了模型间从众性的差异及其演化趋势。尽管整体趋同加剧，顶级模型最新一代的偏离可能预示战略调整。模型比人类更集中于少数高频答案，反映出训练数据、优化目标和部署策略的深层影响。

Abstract: When a language model must pick one answer from a large space of equally valid options, which does it pick -- and how often is it the same answer every other model picks? Asked to "pick a word -- any word," 44 models chose "serendipity" 41% of the time. We characterize this convergence with a deliberately minimal instrument: 31 single-turn prompts, each naming a category with many valid one-word answers ("Name a tree."), asked four times per model with no system prompt. Analysis is exact-match on normalized tokens -- no embeddings, no judge -- at about a dollar per model. That models converge is well documented; our contribution is the instrument itself -- the One-Word Census -- and what it reveals about the structure of the convergence. We score each model by answer-choice surprisal: the average $-\log2$ probability of its answers under the pooled answers of all other models, leave-one-out. Convergence is extreme -- in 7 of 31 categories one answer takes over 80% of all answers -- yet conformity varies more than fourfold across models, and the variation is structured. Persona- and community-tuned models are the most divergent; the newest mainline flagships are the most conformist, producing almost no answer no other model gave. Within four lineages (Claude, GPT, Qwen, Grok) conformity rises with each generation -- but reverses for the latest flagship Claude and GPT models, a possible early signal of repositioning at the top tier. Rankings are robust to roster composition (leave-one-family-out rho = 0.985). Against human category-production norms, the field is more concentrated than people in 18 of 20 shared categories. All prompts, transcripts, and code are public.

</details>


### [137] [Knowledgeless Language Models: Suppressing Parametric Recall for Evidence-Grounded Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12831)
*Roi Cohen,Yvan Carré,Nick Lechtenbörger,Hendrik Droste,Lucas Kerschke,Russa Biswas,Gerard de Melo,Jan Buys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了“无知识”语言模型（KLLM），通过在预训练阶段对命名实体进行匿名化，减少模型对参数化知识的依赖，增强其基于上下文证据进行推理的能力。实验表明，KLLM在多种任务中表现更优，尤其在检索证据不完整的情况下鲁棒性更强，并展现出更好的校准性和拒答行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型过度依赖参数中存储的事实知识，导致在知识过时、不完整或与上下文冲突时表现不可靠。本文旨在探索是否可以通过修改预训练信号，使模型从依赖内部知识转向依赖外部证据进行推理。

Method: 提出KLLM框架，在预训练语料中将所有命名实体匿名化，从而切断模型获取实体相关事实监督的主要途径；在多个模型规模下进行训练，并在问答、事实验证和幻觉检测等任务上评估其性能，特别是在有上下文支持和检索增强的设置下。

Result: KLLM显著降低了闭卷问答中的事实回忆能力，但在提供上下文信息的任务中表现更好；在检索证据不完美的情况下，相比标准模型取得20-25%的相对提升；同时在校准指标（ECE、Brier分数、AUROC）和拒答行为上表现更优。

Conclusion: 抑制预训练期间的实体关联监督可系统性地改变模型的认知行为，使其更依赖外部证据而非内部记忆，从而提高在现实场景下的可靠性；该方法可作为检索增强和工具调用系统的有力补充，构建更可信的基础模型。

Abstract: Language models encode substantial factual knowledge in their parameters, which can lead to unreliable behavior when this knowledge is outdated, incomplete, or misaligned with the provided context. In this work, we study whether modifying the pretraining signal can systematically shift models away from parametric recall and toward evidence-grounded reasoning. We introduce Knowledge--''Less'' Language Models (KLLMs), a fundamentally different epistemic training paradigm for LLMs, which are pretrained on corpora in which named entities are anonymized, thereby removing a primary channel for entity-linked factual supervision. This intervention substantially reduces closed-book factual recall, while often improving performance on tasks where relevant information is provided as context. Across multiple model scales, KLLMs consistently outperform matched baselines on contextual question answering, fact verification, and hallucination detection benchmarks. Crucially, in retrieval-grounded settings with imperfect evidence, KLLMs show improved robustness and achieve up to 20--25\% relative gains over standard language models. They further exhibit better calibration, with improved ECE, Brier score, and AUROC, as well as more reliable abstention behavior. Our results demonstrate that suppressing entity-linked supervision during pretraining induces a shift in epistemic behavior: KLLMs rely less on parametric knowledge and more on external evidence, leading to improved reliability under realistic conditions. This suggests that pretraining-time control over knowledge acquisition can complement retrieval-augmented and tool-based systems by providing a more evidence-sensitive base model.

</details>


### [138] [Can LLMs Write Reliable Rubrics? A Meta-Evaluation for Experiment Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12835)
*Hanhua Hong,Yizhi Li,Jiaoyan Chen,Luu Gia Huy,Sophia Ananiadou,Jung-jae Kim,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统地对基于大语言模型（LLM）生成的评分标准（rubrics）在论文复现任务中的应用进行了元评估，提出将评分标准转化为清单形式，并通过内在和外在指标评估不同生成设置的效果，发现增强型设置能显著提升与真实评分标准的对齐度，接近人类基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于为每篇论文手工构建评分标准需要大量专家投入，限制了如PaperBench等基准的可扩展性，因此亟需自动化生成评分标准的方法以支持大规模、可靠的LLM研究代理评估。

Method: 将评分标准重新设计为清单格式，基于两种主干模型评估四种生成设置；采用语义相似性进行内在评估，使用与真实评分标准的得分对齐程度进行外在评估。

Result: 增强型生成设置显著提升了下游评估与真实评分标准的对齐度，最佳表现接近人类基线，但内在指标提升有限；分析显示LLM生成的评分标准存在过于细致、高分倾向及领域适应性差等问题。

Conclusion: LLM可有效生成用于论文复现评估的评分标准，尤其在外在表现上接近人类水平，但其生成结果仍存在细粒度偏差和适应性局限，需进一步优化以实现可靠的大规模应用。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is a promising approach for assessing open-ended outputs from LLM-based research agents, particularly in paper reproduction, where direct paper-to-repository comparison is prone to hallucination. However, constructing paper-specific rubrics requires substantial expert effort, limiting the scalability of benchmarks such as PaperBench. In this work, we present, to our knowledge, the first systematic meta-evaluation of LLM-generated rubrics for paper reproduction. We reformulate rubrics into a checklist-style format and evaluate four generation settings across two backbone models. We meta-evaluate generated rubrics intrinsically by semantic similarity and extrinsically by score alignment with ground-truth rubrics. Our results show that the augmented settings substantially improves downstream evaluation alignment, with the strongest setting approaching the human baseline, while intrinsic gains are more modest. Further analyses reveal that LLM-generated rubrics are often overly fine-grained, biased toward high scores, and less adaptive to paper domains, highlighting both the affordances and limitations.

</details>


### [139] [Evaluating Large Language Models on Misconceptions in Multi-Turn Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12884)
*Monica Munnangi,Saiph Savage*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ThReadMed-QA，一个包含2,437个患者-医生对话线程的多轮医疗对话数据集，用于评估大语言模型在多轮对话中识别和纠正医学误解的能力。研究发现，即使是最先进的模型，在后续对话轮次中的表现也显著下降，揭示了当前模型在持续纠正错误信念方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架无法捕捉大语言模型在多轮医疗对话中对误解的识别与纠正能力，尤其是在误解可能随对话发展而演变的情况下。因此，需要构建新的数据集和评估方法来系统研究模型在此类场景下的表现。

Method: 构建了一个名为ThReadMed-QA的多轮医疗对话数据集，包含来自AskDocs的真实患者互动数据，并采用基于评分标准的LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估五个大语言模型在识别和纠正医学误解方面的能力。同时进行 oracle 分析，比较模型输出与真实医生回应的影响。

Result: 前沿模型如GPT-5和Claude-Haiku在首次提问时能以约85%的准确率纠正误解，但在两轮后续提问后下降至约50%。误差传播是性能下降的主要原因，即使在理想上下文条件下表现仍不完美。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在多轮医疗对话中难以稳定维持对误解的纠正能力，容易导致不一致甚至危险的建议，凸显了开发更适应多轮交互的评估体系和改进模型推理一致性的迫切需求。

Abstract: Patients seeking medical information often ask questions that embed incorrect assumptions or misconceptions. In such cases, safe medical communication requires not only answering the question, but identifying and correcting the underlying false belief. These interactions naturally unfold over multiple turns, a pattern now mirrored in interactions with LLMs. Yet current evaluation frameworks do not capture model behavior in these settings, where misconceptions can emerge, persist, or evolve over the course of a conversation. Whether LLMs can reliably correct such misconceptions over time remains largely unexamined. To study this, we introduce ThReadMed-QA, a multi-turn medical dialogue dataset of 2,437 patient-physician conversation threads comprising 8,204 question-answer pairs, derived from real patient interactions on AskDocs. This dataset enables systematic evaluation of whether models can detect and correct misconceptions under a multi-turn context. We evaluate five LLMs using a rubric-based LLM-as-a-Judge framework that scores responses based on their ability to identify and correct misconceptions. Our experiments reveal a consistent pattern: even frontier models that can address misconceptions in a single interaction degrade substantially over subsequent turns. GPT-5 and Claude-Haiku correct these false presuppositions around 85% on initial questions but drop to roughly 50% within two follow-ups. An oracle analysis replacing prior model outputs with physician responses shows that much of the degradation is driven by error propagation, while performance remains imperfect even under correct context. Even when models tend to correct misconceptions initially, their performance degrades substantially over later turns, leading to inconsistent and potentially unsafe guidance in patient-facing settings and highlighting the need for evaluation frameworks that capture multi-turn behavior.

</details>


### [140] [LLM Judges Can Be Too Generous When There Is No Reference Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12885)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sowmya Vajjala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在无参考答案设置下，大语言模型（LLM）作为评判者评估开放性回答的可靠性问题，提出通过校准和敏感性实验评估其性能，并发现引入参考答案可显著改变判断结果，建议在实际应用前对LLM评判者进行参考感知校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨LLM评判者在缺乏真实答案的情况下是否能可靠评估模型输出，揭示其潜在偏差。

Method: 采用两阶段实验：一是校准实验评估评判模型对任务知识的掌握；二是敏感性实验分析参考答案在提示中的存在与位置对其判断的影响。实验涵盖三种语言，并与人工标注子集进行比较。

Result: 发现所评估的LLM评判者在无参考答案时倾向于高估错误回答的正确性；加入参考答案后，在某些设置中高达85%的判断被反转；这些变化更贴近人工判断。

Conclusion: 使用LLM作为评判者前需进行参考感知校准，以提高其在无参考环境下的可靠性；本文提供了可用于其他任务的LLM评判者校准方法框架。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly being used to evaluate open-ended model responses, often in no-reference settings where a ground-truth answer is unavailable. However, can they reliably assess in such evaluation setups? We explore this question in this paper through a two stage pipeline with a) calibration experiments that assess the judge model's knowledge of the task it is evaluating, and b) sensitivity experiments that assess how the judge model's performance is impacted by the presence and positioning of the reference answer in the prompt. Across experiments covering three languages, we show that the judge models we evaluated tend to over-credit incorrect answers in the absence of a reference answer, and adding reference answer information to the prompt flips the judge model's correct/incorrect decisions by as much as 85% in some experimental settings. Comparison with a subset of human annotations shows that these reference-driven changes generally align with human judgments. Our results emphasize the need for calibrating the LLM judges with a sample with reference-aware evaluation before using them in reference-free setups reliably, and our methodology provides a blueprint for researchers and practitioners in doing such calibration of LLM judges for other tasks.

</details>


### [141] [The Illusion of Robustness: Aggregate Accuracy Hides Prediction Flips under Task-Irrelevant Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12963)
*Yanzhe Zhang,Sanmi Koyejo,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，尽管大语言模型在整体准确率上对无关上下文表现出鲁棒性，但在个体样本层面存在显著不稳定性，随机字符组成的伪词也可能改变模型预测，揭示出被聚合指标掩盖的尾部风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示大语言模型在面对长且部分无关上下文时的真实鲁棒性，特别是在聚合指标可能掩盖个体不稳定性的情况下。

Method: 通过在基准问题前添加无关上下文（包括语义无意义的伪词）进行控制实验，评估多个模型和数据集上的性能变化，并分析上下文类型、长度、测试时计算资源和模型发展阶段的影响。

Result: 发现尽管整体准确率稳定，但少量样本的预测会因无关上下文而显著改变，这种影响具有双向性（部分变差、部分改善），且受影响样本具有模型特异性。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率可能掩盖语言模型的上下文诱导尾部风险，需引入逐样本的可靠性评估来更全面地衡量模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow more capable, they are increasingly deployed in context-rich settings where task inputs are often accompanied by long, partially irrelevant context. In a controlled setting, we find that state-of-the-art models often appear robust to task-irrelevant context at the aggregate level: prepending it to benchmark questions causes little change in overall accuracy. This aggregate stability, however, masks significant per-example instability. Even semantically meaningless pseudo-words, formed by randomly combining characters, can markedly shift model predictions on a small fraction of examples, degrading performance on some while improving it on others. This two-sided effect holds consistently across a wide range of models and datasets, yet the affected examples are largely model-specific. We further show that this instability is modulated by context type, context length, test-time compute, and model development stage. Together, our findings reveal context-induced tail risks concealed by aggregate accuracy, motivating per-example reliability evaluation of language models.

</details>


### [142] [PalmClaw: A Native On-Device Agent Framework for Mobile Phones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13027)
*Hongru Cai,Yongqi Li,Ran Wei,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PalmClaw是一个开源的移动端LLM代理框架，直接在手机上运行，通过将设备能力封装为具有明确参数和执行边界的工具，提升任务成功率与执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动端代理多依赖GUI操作，导致动作序列冗长、无法直接访问设备功能且执行边界模糊，限制了效率与可控性。

Method: 设计并实现PalmClaw框架，原生运行于移动设备，将设备能力抽象为结构化工具，支持显式参数传递与结果返回，并管理会话、内存、技能和代理循环。

Result: 实验显示相比最强基线，任务成功率相对提升11.5%，完成时间减少94.9%，设置负担更低，并通过执行轨迹验证了清晰的执行边界。

Conclusion: PalmClaw通过原生集成和结构化工具设计，实现了高效、可控的移动端代理，推动LLM代理在移动场景下的实际应用。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have moved beyond generating responses to executing multi-step tasks by calling tools, observing the results, and iteratively deciding the next action. Most agent systems run on desktops or servers, which support tool use and task automation. Mobile devices are also important agent environments because they are widely accessible and contain users' data, sensors, and daily-use applications. Existing mobile agents mainly operate smartphones through graphical user interface (GUI) actions such as tapping, swiping, and typing, which often form long, interface-dependent sequences, cannot directly access device capabilities, and make execution boundaries difficult to define. We present \textbf{PalmClaw}, an open-source agent framework that runs natively on mobile phones and manages the sessions, memory, skills, tools, and agent loop directly on the device. PalmClaw exposes device capabilities as device tools with explicit arguments, structured results, and clearly defined execution boundaries. This design enables agents to use mobile capabilities directly while keeping each action explicit and controlled. Experiments show an 11.5\% relative improvement in task success and a 94.9\% reduction in completion time over the strongest baseline, with lower setup burden and traces illustrating how execution boundaries are applied. Code is available at https://github.com/ModalityDance/PalmClaw.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [EFLUX: Elastic Multi-Robot Formation Navigation and Adaptation with Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12050)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Yuwei Wu,Guangyao Shi,Jonathan Diller,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EFLUX是一种基于几何的LLM智能体框架，用于多机器人编队在复杂环境中的自适应导航，能够联合推理形变与重组行为，实现安全、连续且弹性的运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将形变和重组行为建模为独立过程或依赖手工规则，缺乏统一的几何判断标准，导致在复杂环境中易出现死锁和路径次优问题。

Method: 提出EFLUX框架，通过提取结构化场景表示，利用大语言模型（LLM）联合推理机器人的形变（如缩放、剪切）与重组（如分裂、合并）行为，并通过闭环流程生成、验证和修正每个机器人的航点。

Result: 仿真与实物实验表明，EFLUX能在受限环境中有效实现多机器人编队的安全导航，减少死锁与导航失败，优于基线方法，同时保持团队协调性。

Conclusion: EFLUX通过几何感知与LLM协同推理，实现了多机器人编队在复杂环境下的弹性自适应导航，提升了鲁棒性与自主决策能力。

Abstract: Multi-robot teams operating in confined or cluttered environments must adapt both their formation geometry and group topology to navigate through complex obstacles. This adaptation requires two complementary behaviors: deformation, where the team continuously reshapes its geometry while remaining connected, and reconfiguration, where robots split into subgroups or merge back into a single formation. Existing methods often model these behaviors independently, connect them through handcrafted rules, or lack explicit geometric criteria for determining when each behavior should be invoked. However, challenging environments may require online changes in formation shape, connectivity, and effective team composition, making decoupled or rule-based approaches prone to suboptimal trajectories and deadlock. We propose EFLUX, a geometry-grounded LLM agentic framework for automatic and elastic multi-robot formation navigation. EFLUX extracts a structured scene representation and uses an LLM to reason jointly over both deformation actions, such as scaling and shearing, and reconfiguration actions, such as splitting and merging. These strategies are then translated into executable per-robot waypoints through a closed-loop generation, verification, and correction pipeline. Simulation and hardware experiments show that EFLUX enables safe, continuous, and elastic formation navigation in constrained environments, reducing deadlock and navigation failures compared with baselines while maintaining coherent multi-robot coordination.

</details>


### [144] [Enabling 24-hour Agricultural Robotics: Unsupervised Day-to-Night Cross-Modal Image Translation for Nighttime Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12065)
*Robel Mamo,Rajitha de Silva,Grzegorz Cielniak,Taeyeong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无监督图像翻译框架，将白天的RGB图像转换为夜间近红外（NIR）图像，用于农业机器人夜间视觉导航，并发布了首个夜间农业视觉导航数据集AgriNight。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业机器人视觉导航系统多依赖白天数据，缺乏夜间标注数据，限制了24小时自主作业能力。夜间部署具有监测、采摘和害虫检测等优势，但缺少适用于夜间的高质量标注图像数据集。

Method: 提出一种基于CLIP预训练模型的无监督图像翻译框架，将白天RGB图像转为夜间NIR图像，引入可见性掩码以模拟NIR照明范围限制，并利用白天语义标签直接训练夜间感知模型。

Result: 在AgriNight数据集上优于现有图像翻译方法，生成图像质量更高，下游语义分割性能提升；实现实时夜间自主导航，验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了夜间农业视觉导航中数据稀缺问题，实现了无需像素级标注的跨昼夜域适应，推动了全天候农业机器人的发展。

Abstract: While visual navigation has been extensively studied in agricultural robotics, most existing systems assume daytime conditions. In fact, deploying autonomous robots at night offers significant advantages, including 24-hour crop and soil monitoring, fruit harvesting, and nocturnal pest detection. Modern vision-based systems, however, rely heavily on large-scale well-annotated image datasets, which remains challenging to obtain for nighttime operation scenarios. To address this, we propose an unsupervised image translation framework that converts daytime plant-row RGB images into near-infrared (NIR) nighttime counterparts without requiring pixel-to-pixel supervision. This enables the direct reuse of daytime semantic labels for training nighttime perception models. In particular, by incorporating a pre-trained Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) model, the proposed framework is designed to preserve semantic consistency during day-to-night translation. Additionally, a visibility mask is introduced to account for the limited effective range of NIR illumination in nighttime scenes. We conduct comparative evaluations with state-of-the-art image translation baselines and demonstrate higher image qualities, as supported by improved performance in downstream semantic segmentation for nighttime visual navigation. For evaluation, we utilize AgriNight--a novel dataset comprising 428 daytime and 549 nighttime images collected using night-vision-equipped mobile robots in agricultural fields and manually annotated with pixel-wise semantic labels--and introduce it as the first benchmark for nighttime agricultural visual navigation. We also perform real-time autonomous navigation experiments with a physical robot operating at night. The data and code are available at: https://github.com/mamorobel/AgriNight.

</details>


### [145] [Robust In-Hand Manipulation via Priors in Reinforcement Learning and Mechanical Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12105)
*Yifei Chen,Shihan Lu,Ed Colgate,Kevin Lynch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理先验的强化学习方法，用于提升多指机器人手在无外部感知条件下的手中滚动操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无外部感知的手内操作中，由于手指-物体接触的不确定性和重力干扰，保持稳定且高效的抓握极具挑战性；现有强化学习方法未充分考虑维持良好抓握状态的重要性。

Method: 引入两种互补的物理先验：一种是基于经典抓握分析的全局抓握质量先验，用作密集奖励塑形项；另一种是基于指尖曲率的局部接触几何先验，通过机械结构设计优化接触界面。

Result: 在三种不同物体和四种手掌姿态下进行实验，结果表明所提方法显著提高了旋转效率、抓握稳定性和抗干扰能力。

Conclusion: 将物理先验同时嵌入学习过程和指尖形态设计中，可有效提升任务鲁棒性和仿真到现实的迁移能力。

Abstract: In-hand manipulation without external sensing is challenging due to uncertainties from finger-object contacts and disturbances by gravity. While reinforcement learning has shown promise in learning complex finger gaiting, existing approaches do not prioritize maintaining well-conditioned grasps for sustained manipulation. We introduce two complementary physics priors for robust in-hand rolling: a global grasp-quality prior derived from classical grasp analysis and a local contact-geometry prior based on fingertip curvature. The grasp-quality prior is used as a dense reward-shaping term that encourages well-distributed contacts with improved worst-case wrench resistance. The contact-geometry prior is expressed in the fingertip geometry that mechanically shapes the contact interface toward task-aligned rolling while reducing off-axis drift. We evaluate the effect of these priors on learning in-hand rolling manipulation for a multifingered robotic hand manipulating three different objects at four palm orientations. Results show significant improvement in rotation efficiency, grasp stability, and disturbance rejection, suggesting that physics priors embedded in both learning and fingertip morphology improve task robustness and sim-to-real transfer. An overview video can be found at https://youtu.be/pdd1wHxQnJM?si=dM-U5kiiPTYsk3Pk.

</details>


### [146] [GaitSpan: Growing Humanoid Locomotion from Walking to Running](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12114)
*Kwan-Yee Lin,Zilin Wang,Janelle J. Liu,Stella X. Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaitSpan是一种扩展预训练步行策略以实现从行走、慢跑到跑步连续运动的框架，通过节奏生成、步幅塑造和残差适应三方面实现对速度、地形和形态变化的零样本迁移与强健控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在扩展人形机器人步态多样性时灵活性不足，难以适应连续速度指令、不同地形和形态变化，且常需从头学习或依赖专家策略与动作模仿。本文旨在利用已有的基础步行技能作为种子，发展出可扩展的快速运动能力，避免重复学习基础平衡与协调机制。

Method: 提出GaitSpan框架，包含三个核心组件：1）节奏生成——通过多个内部时钟调制冻结的步行策略，学习命令条件下的动作组合；2）步幅塑造——基于弹簧负载倒立摆动力学设计物理启发的目标函数，鼓励适合高速运动的动力学模式；3）残差适应——捕捉前两阶段未覆盖的运动细节。整个策略在单一命令条件下统一控制多种步态。

Result: GaitSpan首次实现了单个策略下覆盖行走至类跑步连续速度范围的人形运动，在多个模拟环境与真实地形上实现零样本迁移，并支持跨形态部署。相比多专家或多模态模仿基线方法，训练更快且步态性能更强。

Conclusion: 通过将基础步行作为可复用的种子技能，GaitSpan实现了高效、灵活且鲁棒的步态扩展，为人形机器人技能增长提供了新范式。

Abstract: A humanoid that can walk should not relearn locomotion from scratch to jog or run. Yet current approaches often obtain gait diversity by prescribing gait schedules, imitating motion clips, training experts to switch between or distilling skills into one policy. These strategies can produce impressive behaviors, but offer limited flexibility across continuous speed commands, terrains, and morphologies. We study skill growth with GaitSpan, a framework that expands a pretrained, basic walking policy into faster locomotion. It treats walking as a seed skill: reusable motor structure for balance, support, body coordination, and contact transition that can be regenerated at new rhythms, extended into longer/higher strides, and corrected by residual adaptation. This expansion has three aspects: 1) rhythm generation, which modulates the frozen walking policy with multiple internal clocks and learns command-conditioned combinations of the resulting canonical actions; 2) stride shaping, which rewards dynamic locomotion patterns appropriate for higher commanded speeds using a physically grounded objective inspired by spring-loaded inverted pendulum dynamics; and 3) residual adaptation, which captures motion details not accounted for by rhythm generation or stride shaping. GaitSpan is the first to deliver a single command-conditioned humanoid policy that spans walking, jogging, and running-like regimes covering a continuous speed range, transfers across morphologies, and deploys zero-shot on unseen sim-to-sim, and real-world terrains. Compared with baselines either trained with multi-experts or imitation from humans, it learns faster and achieves stronger gait performance.

</details>


### [147] [A Behavioral State Vocabulary in Sony ERS-111 R-CODE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12115)
*Christopher A. Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过对索尼ERS-111 AIBO的R-CODE样本集生成的行为图进行语料库级分析，揭示了多种行为程序背后共有的、基于状态的紧凑控制语法，并探讨其在构建新行为模块中的抽象价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解AIBO机器人样本行为脚本中潜在的结构共性，而非孤立地分析单个脚本，以揭示设计模式和可复用的控制逻辑。

Method: 采用语料库级别的分析方法，比较跨样本集中命名状态的共现模式，聚合生成整体行为结构图，识别出核心控制词汇和常见流程。

Result: 发现许多看似不同的行为例程实际上由一套紧凑的具身化语法构成，核心包括初始化、感知、迭代动作、同步和恢复等状态；形成了一个可概括的控制结构模型。

Conclusion: 基于状态的抽象不仅有助于历史分析和理解原有程序设计，还可作为在资源受限的原生机器人系统上构建新封装行为例程的有效中间表示。

Abstract: This paper presents a corpus-level analysis of generated behavior diagrams derived from Sony's R-CODE sample distribution for the ERS-111 AIBO. Rather than reading each script in isolation, the study compares named states across the corpus to identify the recurring control vocabulary that structures the sample set. The resulting aggregate shows that many superficially different routines are built from a compact embodied grammar centered on initialization, sensing, iterative action, synchronization, and recovery. In addition to historical analysis, the paper argues that this form of state-based abstraction is useful as an intermediate representation for constructing new encapsulated behavior routines, especially on constrained native robotic systems where deterministic control, direct hardware access, and modular behavioral composition remain important.

</details>


### [148] [More than a Manipulator: Planning Propellant-Free Attitude Maneuvers for Free-Floating Spacecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12130)
*Harsh G. Bhundiya,Avi Soval,Keenan Albee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了利用机械臂运动实现无推进剂的航天器姿态控制，通过轨迹优化方法结合关键关节和避障约束，展示了多种航天器-机械臂系统的复杂转向与消旋轨迹，并证明机械臂可作为冗余或主要的姿态控制系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统航天器姿态控制依赖动量交换装置或消耗性推进器，而机械臂通常被视为扰动源；本文旨在探索将机械臂主动用于姿态控制，以节省推进剂并提升任务灵活性。

Method: 构建了一个包含关节限制和碰撞规避约束的轨迹优化问题，并采用内点法求解非线性规划问题，实现航天器-机械臂系统的协同姿态控制。

Result: 成功演示了不同构型和质量特性的航天器-机械臂系统的复杂姿态机动轨迹，并通过动量与力矩包络线对比显示，机械臂具备与反作用轮阵列相当的控制能力。

Conclusion: 机械臂可作为多用途姿态控制执行机构，在轨组装与制造等任务中具有广泛应用前景，尤其适用于抓取大质量比载荷的情形。

Abstract: Spacecraft attitude control is traditionally achieved using momentum exchange devices or propellant-consuming thrusters. Meanwhile, a growing number of missions require robotic manipulators, which are typically treated as disturbance sources to be rejected rather than as actuators for spacecraft reorientation. This work investigates the use of manipulator motions for propellant-free attitude control by formulating a trajectory optimization problem with critical joint and collision avoidance constraints. Using an interior point solver for the resulting nonlinear program, complex slew and detumble trajectories are demonstrated for a range of spacecraft-manipulator systems with varying kinematic complexity and mass properties. The achievable control authority is compared directly with that of reaction wheel arrays via momentum and torque envelopes, demonstrating the potential for manipulators to serve as redundant or even primary attitude control systems. This work provides a framework for using manipulators as multipurpose attitude control actuators, with particularly promising applications in in-space assembly and manufacturing when grasping payloads with high relative mass fractions.

</details>


### [149] [Analysis of Mutual and Referential Human and Robot Gazes in a Collaborative Word Association Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12181)
*Jens V. Rüppel,Tim Schreiter,Andrey Rudenko,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在认知要求较高的协作词语联想游戏中，机器人凝视如何影响人类的视觉注意力及确认性凝视行为。实验比较了机器人使用互视与指示性凝视两种条件，并通过眼动追踪分析参与者注视模式。结果显示，机器人凝视方向不影响参与者对机器人提出词语的首次注视时间；但在对话中包含确认请求时，参与者更频繁地看向机器人。研究发现，在高任务负荷下，言语交流的影响超过了指示性凝视的作用，为设计协作任务中的机器人凝视与话轮转换行为提供了重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数关于人机对话中凝视行为的研究集中在面对面的社交对话上，而在高要求的任务型互动中机器人凝视的作用尚不清楚。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索在需要高效协调与沟通的协作任务中，机器人凝视如何引导人类注意力及其社会信号功能。

Method: 让参与者与一个由大语言模型驱动的NAO机器人进行协作性的词语联想游戏。实验设置两种条件：机器人表现出互视和指示性凝视。使用眼动眼镜记录参与者的视线数据，并结合语音片段、关键事件和兴趣区域进行多模态交互分析。

Result: 1. 机器人凝视方向不影响参与者对机器人提议词语的首次注视时间；2. 当参与者的语句中包含确认请求时，他们更频繁地注视机器人；3. 在高认知负荷的任务中，言语内容主导了交互过程，削弱了指示性凝视的效果。

Conclusion: 在高任务需求的协作对话中，尽管机器人凝视未能显著引导人类对特定对象的早期注意力，但它仍作为社会线索被用于确认性交流。这表明在设计任务导向的人机交互系统时，应优先考虑言语协调机制，同时合理整合凝视行为以支持自然的话轮管理和意图表达。

Abstract: Robot gaze is a major component of human-robot dialogue coordination. Most studies of gaze in human-robot dialogue focus on face-to-face social conversations, but little is known about gaze in demanding task-focused interactions. In this paper, we investigate how the gaze of a robot game partner affects human visual attention and if humans tend to direct confirmation-seeking gazes towards the robot. In our study, we let participants play a collaborative word association game with a NAO robot acting as an embodied, LLM-driven conversational partner. Our experiments are conducted under two conditions, which implement mutual and referential gazes of the robot respectively. We record participants' gaze using eye tracking glasses and analyze the interactions using gaze coordinates, speech segments, key events and areas of interests. We find that robot gaze orientation does not affect the time to first fixation on words the robot proposed. We also find that participants gaze more often at the robot when their dialogue line contains confirmation requests, compared to when it does not. Our results indicate (likely also due to the cognitively demanding nature of the game) that the verbal aspect of this task overshadows the effects of referential robot gaze. These findings offer valuable insights for designing and validating robot gaze and turn-taking behavior in collaborative tasks which require coordination and efficient communication.

</details>


### [150] [Contract-Grounded Behavior Tree Synthesis via Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12220)
*Jonathan Salfity,Robert Blake Anderson,Mitch Pryor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于契约的机器人行为树（BT）合成架构，通过从机器人端的MCP服务器获取技能库和结构约束，实现自然语言到可执行BT的可靠转换，支持非专家用户在不了解底层细节的情况下对模拟和真实机器人下达指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的BT生成方法依赖提示词设计者了解机器人能力，缺乏对技能和结构的有效接地，导致部署脆弱；本文旨在使BT生成过程自动适配机器人的实际执行能力。

Method: 提出一种契约接地的BT合成框架：编码代理通过查询机器人侧的Model Context Protocol（MCP）服务器获取明确的契约（包括技能库、允许的BT操作符和可选的组合模板），再生成符合运行时约束的行为树，并由机器人端验证门控确保正确性。

Result: 在PyRoboSim模拟环境中的110个任务和Husarion Panther真实机器人上的14个任务中评估了两种LLM（Sonnet 4.6和Gemma31b）。结果显示该方法实现了接近完美的BT验证通过率和高任务成功率，组合模板显著提升了小模型在反应式控制流任务上的表现，且架构可迁移至运行不透明Nav2栈的真实硬件。

Conclusion: 契约接地的合成架构有效解耦了用户意图与机器人实现细节，提升了LLM生成行为树的可靠性与可部署性，适用于非专家操作员与复杂机器人系统的交互。

Abstract: Synthesizing deployable robot behavior trees (BTs) from natural language (NL) requires grounding to ensure every generated BT references only skills a robot can actually execute. Existing LLM-based BT synthesis approaches often place this grounding responsibility on the prompt author. This makes deployment brittle when the author does not know which skills the robot can execute, how those skills are parameterized, or how the robot runtime software constrains valid BT structure. This paper proposes a contract-grounded BT synthesis architecture in which a coding agent queries a robot-side Model Context Protocol (MCP) server to retrieve an explicit contract consisting of a skill library, permitted BT operators, and optional BT composition templates, before synthesizing a BT for validation and execution. In our framework, non-expert operators issue NL commands without knowledge of robot implementation details, while a robot runtime validation gate enforces correctness before execution. We evaluate two LLMs, a closed model (Sonnet 4.6) and a smaller open-source model (Gemma4:31b), across 110 simulated tasks in PyRoboSim and 14 tasks on a physical Husarion Panther robot. Results show that contract grounding enables near-perfect BT validation and high task success, that BT composition templates substantially recover success on reactive control-flow tasks for the smaller model, and that the architecture transfers to physical hardware running a Nav2 stack opaque to both operator and agent.

</details>


### [151] [DiffRadar: Differentiable Physics-Aware Radar SLAM with Gaussian Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12265)
*Gaurav Bagwe,Xiaoyong Yuan,Yongji Wu,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffRadar是一种实时雷达SLAM系统，通过将雷达观测建模为可微分、物理感知的高斯场，直接在信号域优化机器人位姿与场景结构，显著提升了轨迹精度和地图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达SLAM系统依赖于离散化的雷达热图进行扫描匹配，破坏了几何连续性且未能充分捕捉雷达传感的关键特性，导致在退化或动态环境中位姿估计不稳定和建图性能下降。

Method: DiffRadar将场景表示为各向异性的高斯基元，并通过可微分的雷达前向模型在距离-方位和多普勒-方位空间中渲染雷达测量值，实现从原始雷达数据对机器人位姿和场景结构的联合优化。

Result: 在Radarize基准和自建压力测试套件上评估显示，DiffRadar大幅降低了轨迹误差（尤其在特征稀疏的走廊环境中），地图一致性提高一倍以上，并保持70 FPS的实时性能。

Conclusion: 直接在信号域建模雷达观测可显著提升雷达-only SLAM的鲁棒性和一致性，适用于移动平台。

Abstract: Radar sensing is increasingly used in mobile systems because it operates reliably under poor lighting, adverse weather, and privacy-sensitive settings where cameras and LiDAR often fail. However, most existing radar SLAM systems estimate motion through scan matching on discretized radar heatmaps, which breaks geometric continuity and fails to capture key radar sensing properties, often leading to unstable pose estimation and degraded mapping in regenerate or dynamically changing environments. We present DiffRadar, a real-time radar SLAM system that models radar observations as a differentiable, physics-aware Gaussian field rather than discrete scans. DiffRadar represents the scene as anisotropic Gaussian primitives and renders radar measurements in range-azimuth and Doppler-azimuth spaces through a differentiable radar forward model, enabling joint optimization of robot pose and scene structure directly from radar measurements. We implement DiffRadar on commodity FMCW radar hardware and evaluate it on both the public Radarize benchmark and a controlled stress-test suite that targets common radar SLAM failure modes, including corridor degeneracy, motion regime transitions, dynamic clutter, and long-horizon loop closures. DiffRadar achieves substantial reductions in trajectory error on the benchmark, with especially large gains under feature-poor corridor motion, while more than doubling map consistency and maintaining real-time performance at 70 FPS. These results show that modeling radar observations directly in the signal domain enables substantially more robust and consistent radar-only SLAM for mobile platforms.

</details>


### [152] [Flatness-Preserving Residual Learning for Real-Time Tight Quadrotor Formation Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12275)
*Pei-An Hsieh,Fengjun Yang,Nikolai Matni,M. Ani Hsieh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息残差动力学学习框架，用于捕捉四旋翼飞行器编队中的复杂空气动力学相互作用，保持系统微分平坦性，并实现高效、稳定的紧密编队飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧密编队飞行的四旋翼会受到强烈的空气动力学干扰（如下洗气流），可能导致碰撞，传统方法难以建模和补偿这些复杂效应。

Method: 结合物理先验构建残差动力学学习模型，保持系统的微分平坦性，进而设计基于反馈线性化的控制器，利用前馈补偿抵消气动干扰。

Result: 硬件实验显示跟踪误差平均降低31%，性能媲美非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），但计算量减少一个数量级。仅需30秒训练数据和5ms控制周期即可实现稳定紧密编队飞行。

Conclusion: 该轻量化方法在极低计算资源和少量训练数据下实现了高保真的气动补偿，首次证明了高效、稳定的紧密编队飞行可在计算受限平台上实现。

Abstract: Quadrotors flying in tight formations are severely affected by turbulent aerodynamic interactions, such as downwash, that can cause catastrophic collisions if left unmodeled. To compensate for these effects, we propose a physics-informed residual dynamics learning framework that captures complex aerodynamic interactions while ensuring the joint multi-quadrotor system remains differentially flat. We leverage this preserved flatness to design a computationally efficient feedback linearization controller that is easily tunable with linear control techniques and cancels aerodynamic disturbances via feedforward compensation. Hardware experiments demonstrate our framework reduces average tracking errors by 31% compared to nominal baselines. Crucially, our lightweight approach matches the tracking performance of state-of-the-art nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) while requiring an order of magnitude less computation. We are the first to show that stable, tight formation flight can be achieved with under 30 seconds of training data and a 5ms loop rate, unlocking high-fidelity aerodynamic compensation for compute-constrained flight stacks.

</details>


### [153] [Reducing Temporal Redundancy for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12287)
*Yuzhou Wu,Yuxin Zheng,Muchun Niu,Yishan Yang,Tianhao Liu,hanwen kang,Jiajian Jing,Linfeng Zhang,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种系统级加速策略，通过减少感知和动作生成中的计算量，显著降低视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的推理延迟，实现实时操作下的高效机器人操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型存在高推理延迟问题，主要源于连续帧的重复视觉编码和基于扩散策略的多步迭代采样，限制了其实时部署能力。

Method: 在感知方面，仅对动态场景区域对应的token进行增量更新，而非重新编码整个帧；在策略方面，通过面向效率的训练将扩散采样压缩为紧凑的2步调度，保持动作精度的同时提升速度。

Result: 在Libero、RobotWin和真实机器人平台上实验表明，相比现有方法实现了两倍以上的加速，同时保持高性能，在通用操作基准上达到高达98%的成功率。

Conclusion: 该系统级优化方法有效解决了VLA模型的时间冗余问题，兼顾推理速度与控制精度，推动其向实时机器人控制的实际应用迈进。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit strong generalization for robotic manipulation, yet their high inference latency limits real time deployment. We identify two primary sources of temporal redundancy in existing VLA pipelines: repeated visual encoding of highly similar consecutive frames and multi step iterative sampling in diffusion based policies. To address this, we propose a system level acceleration strategy that reduces computation in both perception and action generation. On the perception side, we incrementally update only tokens corresponding to dynamic scene regions instead of re-encoding entire frames. On the policy side, we compress diffusion sampling into a compact 2-step schedule through efficiency oriented training while preserving action precision. Experiments on Libero, RobotWin, and Real Robot Platforms demonstrate over 2 times speedup while maintaining high performance, achieving up to 98% success rate on general manipulation benchmarks. Our codes will be released on Github.

</details>


### [154] [VistaVLA: Geometry- and Semantic-Aware 3D Gaussian-Grounded VLA for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12356)
*Mohan Liu,Zhihao Gu,Xuanyu Chen,Haitian Zhang,Kaimin Mao,Yan Wu,Wei-Yun Yau,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VistaVLA提出了一种两阶段框架，通过3D高斯基元构建几何与语义感知的3D认知表征，并利用Merge-then-Query机制压缩为紧凑上下文令牌，显著提升了视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人操作中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型缺乏显式的场景级3D表示，限制了其对空间布局和几何约束的推理能力；尽管已有工作引入3D线索，但仍缺少高层次的3D语义 grounding。受人类认知中3D语义认知地图启发，本文旨在构建兼具几何与语义感知的3D表征以增强VLA模型的空间推理能力。

Method: VistaVLA首先将多视角视觉-语言特征提升至3D高斯基元，形成几何锚定的语义令牌，实现视图一致的空间 grounding 与2D特征空间的对齐；随后提出Merge-then-Query（MtQ）机制，将密集的高斯基元压缩为极简的、空间信息丰富的上下文令牌，在保留关键3D结构和语义的同时实现99%的令牌缩减。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界环境中进行了广泛评估，结果表明VistaVLA有效提升了性能：在七个真实任务上成功率提高22.8%，在具有挑战性的分布外任务上相比VLA-Adapter基线提升30.0%。

Conclusion: VistaVLA通过构建可学习的3D语义认知地图并高效压缩为策略学习可用的上下文令牌，显著增强了VLA模型的空间与语义推理能力，为机器人操作提供了更强大、鲁棒的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful end-to-end paradigm for robotic manipulation by mapping language instructions and 2D visual inputs directly to actions. However, these models lack an explicit, scene-level 3D representation, limiting their ability to reason over spatial layouts and geometric constraints. While recent efforts incorporate explicit 3D cues, such as depth maps or point clouds, to improve geometric awareness, they primarily capture low-level structures and lack high-level semantic grounding in 3D space. In human cognition, interaction with the physical world relies on a 3D semantic cognitive map - an internal mental model that integrates spatial layouts with semantic context to enable persistent, viewpoint-invariant reasoning. In light of this, we present VistaVLA, a novel two-stage framework that constructs a geometry- and semantics-aware 3D cognitive representation from 3D Gaussian primitives and grounds it as compact context tokens for VLA policy learning. Specifically, VistaVLA lifts multi-view vision-language features into 3D Gaussian primitives, forming geometry-anchored semantic tokens that align view-consistent spatial grounding with 2D visual feature spaces. To make this 3D representation computationally tractable for effective VLA control, we introduce Merge-then-Query (MtQ), a token summarization mechanism. MtQ compresses dense Gaussian primitives into a highly compact set of spatially informative tokens, achieving a 99% token reduction while preserving action-relevant 3D layouts and semantic context. Extensive evaluations in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness of VistaVLA. Notably, in real-world scenarios, VistaVLA improves success rates by 22.8% across seven real-world tasks and by 30.0% over the VLA-Adapter baseline on challenging out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [155] [StratMamba: Strategic and Reactive Stream Partitioning for Path-Efficient LiDAR-Based Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12370)
*Hung-Chieh Wu,Xiaopan Zhang,Kasra Sinaei,Ryan Abnavi,Kasun Weerakoon,Christopher Bradley,Seyed Fakoorian,Jiachen Li,Donald Ebeigbe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StratMamba是一种双流Mamba架构，用于机器人在复杂环境中高效进行长时序依赖建模，兼顾反应式避障与长期目标规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时序模型在处理长距离导航任务时难以同时兼顾实时避障和战略规划，需要更高效的架构来平衡短期反应与长期记忆。

Method: 提出StratMamba，采用快衰减和慢衰减双记忆结构：快流处理高频LiDAR数据实现快速避障，慢流保留长期目标信息用于全局规划。

Result: 在IsaacLab和Gazebo中验证了多种避障场景，并成功实现Sim-to-Real部署于Unitree GO1四足机器人；相比LSTM、Transformer和Vanilla-Mamba，StratMamba超时率更低，导航速度最快（576步，比Vanilla-Mamba快5.0%），路径效率达0.915，表现最优。

Conclusion: StratMamba通过双流结构有效平衡了反应安全性与战略导航能力，在仿真和真实环境中均展现出更强的鲁棒性和更高的路径优化性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes StratMamba, a dual-stream Mamba-based temporal modeling architecture, to more efficiently capture long-horizon temporal dependencies required for robot navigation in complex and obstacle-rich environments. StratMamba leverages a combination of fast-decay and slow-decay memory architectures, where the fast-decay component processes high-frequency LiDAR data for reactive obstacle avoidance, while the slow-decay component maintains longer-horizon goal information for strategic planning. We perform extensive evaluations of different obstacle avoidance scenarios in IsaacLab and Gazebo, while also validating successful sim-to-real deployment on a Unitree GO1 quadruped robot navigating in the presence of static/dynamic obstacles. Comparisons with other temporal RL baselines, such as LSTM, Transformer, and Vanilla-Mamba, show that our StratMamba achieves exceptional temporal reasoning efficiency with a lower timeout rate, while maintaining the fastest navigation speed (576 median steps, 5.0% better than Vanilla-Mamba). It also achieves the highest path optimality (0.915 path efficiency) across all baselines. Real-world evaluation reveals that StratMamba maintains more robust performance across extended LiDAR ranges compared to vanilla Mamba and the Transformer, demonstrating that dual-stream partitioning effectively balances reactive safety with strategic navigation under challenging sensing conditions.

</details>


### [156] [Model-Based Diffusion Optimal Control for Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12423)
*Zhilin He,Yorai Shaoul,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MDOC的基于模型的扩散规划方法，用于多机器人在连续环境中的运动规划，能够在无需大量演示数据的情况下高效生成动力学可行且无碰撞的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的多机器人运动规划方法依赖大量演示数据，且难以严格满足动力学和安全约束，因此需要一种不依赖数据且能有效保证动态可行性和安全性的新方法。

Method: 提出Model-Based Diffusion Optimal Control (MDOC)，结合已知的动力学模型与控制屏障函数（CBF）约束投影来保证安全性，并通过冲突搜索（Conflict-Based Search）将该机制扩展到多机器人场景中，实现无数据依赖的轨迹采样。

Result: 在仿真实验中，MDOC在采样效率、轨迹平滑性、成功率等方面优于基线方法，同时减少了计算时间并始终生成无碰撞轨迹。

Conclusion: MDOC是一种高效、可扩展的多机器人运动规划方法，通过结合模型先验与约束投影机制，在不依赖训练数据的前提下实现了安全、动态可行的轨迹生成。

Abstract: Multi-Robot Motion Planning in continuous environments, where robots must generate dynamically feasible, collision-free trajectories, is challenging due to the combinatorial growth of the joint trajectory space and the difficulty of enforcing dynamic feasibility and hard safety constraints. Recent approaches recast trajectory planning as probabilistic inference, sampling from a posterior over trajectories using diffusion models whose score functions are learned from demonstration data. While showing promising performance, these approaches are limited: they often rely on sizable demonstration datasets and struggle to rigorously enforce dynamics and hard safety constraints during sampling. To this end, we introduce Model-Based Diffusion Optimal Control (MDOC), a model-based diffusion planner that efficiently produces dynamically feasible trajectories without relying on data. Crucially, we show that MDOC's safety mechanism -- combining known dynamics models with Control Barrier Function-constrained projections -- naturally scales to multi-robot planning settings through Conflict-Based Search. Across simulation experiments, this integrated method consistently outperforms representative baseline planners in sample efficiency, geometric smoothness, and success rate, while reducing computation time and producing collision-free trajectories.

</details>


### [157] [Deployable Human Preference Alignment in Robotics: Learning Representative Rewards from Diverse Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12466)
*Taehyung Kim,Gwangmo Lee,Minjun Chang,Sunghyun Lim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Preference-based REward Clustering (PREC)的新框架，用于从多样用户的二元偏好标签中学习一组紧凑的机器人策略，以解决个体化对齐方法中的稀疏反馈与噪声问题以及单一共享策略难以捕捉异质偏好的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于每个用户提供的偏好反馈稀少且含噪声，传统的逐用户对齐方法不稳定且验证困难；而单一共享策略又无法满足多样化甚至少数用户的偏好需求。因此需要一种兼顾效率与个性化的解决方案。

Method: PREC首先忽略偏好标签，跨用户聚合轨迹数据以学习一个群体层面的共享轨迹编码器；然后基于该表示联合进行用户聚类和每簇奖励模型的学习，最后优化出每个簇对应的策略。这种方法将相似用户分组以缓解单个用户标签不足和噪声影响，并控制模型数量以降低部署前验证成本。

Result: 在多个模拟运动环境中实验表明，PREC能比基线方法更准确地将用户划分为偏好一致的簇；在反馈稀疏且有噪声的情况下，其训练出的策略在三种社会福利指标上均优于现有的单一共享策略方法，甚至超过逐用户对齐方法。

Conclusion: PREC通过结合共享表示学习、用户聚类与集群级奖励建模，有效平衡了个性化与可扩展性，在处理稀疏、噪声偏好反馈时表现出优越性能，为机器人策略的人类偏好对齐提供了实用且高效的框架。

Abstract: Aligning robot policies with human preferences is essential for deployment to diverse end users. In per-user alignment approach, preference feedback is often sparse, so learning becomes unstable and vulnerable to human preference noise, and a growing number of individualized policies makes validation difficult before deployment. A single shared policy approach to user alignment avoids this cost but fails to capture heterogeneous preferences and often neglects minority preferences. To address these challenges, we introduce Preference-based REward Clustering (PREC), a novel framework that learns a compact set of policies from binary preference labels provided by diverse users. From a dataset of user trajectories and their preference labels, PREC first sets the labels aside and aggregates trajectories across users to learn a population-level shared trajectory encoder, alleviating limited per-user coverage and avoiding label noise during representation learning. Using this representation, PREC jointly assigns users to preference-coherent clusters and learns a representative reward model per cluster using preference labels, from which a policy is optimized for each cluster. Clustering similar users compensates for the limited number of labels available from each user and mitigates the effect of label noise. At the same time, maintaining a manageable number of reward models reduces the validation burden at deployment. Experiments across diverse simulated locomotion environments show that PREC groups users who label different trajectory subsets into preference-coherent clusters more accurately than baseline methods. Under sparse and noisy feedback, policies trained with PREC improve all three social welfare metrics over an existing single shared-policy user-alignment approach and even outperform per-user alignment approaches.

</details>


### [158] [Infra-Swarm: Robust Vision-Based Multi-Robot Swarming via Near-Infrared Spectral Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12489)
*Haoyu Chen,Qijin Li,Wanyu Xiang,Xiuxiu Lin,Zian Ning,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于近红外视觉的分布式机器人集群系统Infra-Swarm，通过硬件滤波和低计算开销实现对环境光照变化鲁棒且可扩展的3D邻体感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布式集群依赖主动无线通信或被动视觉，易受带宽限制或环境干扰，需要一种更鲁棒、低功耗且可扩展的感知方案。

Method: 每个机器人配备940nm近红外光源和四个灰度相机，利用窄带滤光片物理抑制99.2%的环境光干扰，并通过光学耀斑的位置与强度信息直接估计邻近机器人的厘米级3D位置。

Result: 系统实现了对环境光照变化具有硬件级鲁棒性的邻体感知，且计算开销极低，适合资源受限的大规模集群应用。

Conclusion: Infra-Swarm为大规模分布式机器人集群提供了一种高效、鲁棒且易于扩展的视觉感知解决方案，适用于复杂光照环境下的实际部署。

Abstract: Distributed swarms typically rely on either active wireless communication or passive vision, and they are frequently hindered by bandwidth constraints or environmental sensitivity. This paper proposes Infra-Swarm, a robust vision-based swarm. Each robot is equipped with a near-infrared light source and four ordinary gray-scale cameras. The Infra-Swarm system directly measures the centimeter-level 3D position of neighbors based on the position (bearing) and intensity (strength) of optical flares in the captured images. By utilizing 940 nm narrow-band filters to physically reject 99.2% of ambient light interference, the perception front-end achieves hardware-level robustness against illumination variations. Furthermore, its minimal computational overhead provides a resilient foundation for the massive scalability of robotic collectives on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [159] [A Bearing-Strength Method for Motion Estimation of Unknown Energy Emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12515)
*Haoyu Chen,Zian Ning,Yin Zhang,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用到达角和信号强度的被动传感器运动估计算法，显著提升了系统可观测性，无需传感器侧向运动，且无需额外成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于纯角度的运动估计方法要求传感器必须有侧向运动以保证可观测性，这在许多实际任务中与期望运动冲突。而接收信号强度信息虽易获取却未被充分利用，因此有必要探索结合强度信息来克服该限制。

Method: 提出一种新的“角度-强度”联合运动估计方法，充分利用到达角和接收信号强度测量值，并进行理论分析以证明其对系统可观测性的提升。

Result: 理论分析表明，新方法不再需要传感器的侧向运动即可保证可观测性；真实实验结果验证了该方法的有效性和理论结论。

Conclusion: 利用接收信号强度可显著增强被动传感器对移动辐射源的运动估计性能，且无需额外硬件成本，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper studies motion estimation of moving energy emitters using passive sensors. The emitters may be light, acoustic, or radio sources. While the bearing vector pointing from the sensor to the emitter can be easily obtained, existing approaches mainly rely on the bearing-only motion estimation method. However, this method suffers from a fundamental limitation that the sensor must have lateral motion to ensure observability. Unfortunately, this lateral motion requirement often conflicts with the sensor's desired motion in many tasks. In this paper, we point out that the received signal strength, which can also be obtained easily in many ways, can greatly enhance motion estimation. Surprisingly, this strength information has not been well explored so far. Here, we propose a new bearing-strength method to fully exploit both the bearing and strength measurements. Our theoretical analysis shows that the system observability is significantly enhanced in the sense that the lateral motion condition is not required anymore. Real-world experimental results verify the proposed method and the theoretical analysis. It is notable that the benefit of the proposed method comes with no additional cost since it simply utilizes the received strength information that has not been fully exploited in the past.

</details>


### [160] [Mind the Gap: Promises and Pitfalls of Hierarchical Planning in LeWorldModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12547)
*Niccolò Caselli,Salvatore Lo Sardo,Francesco Massafra,Ippokratis Pantelidis,Samuele Punzo,Sathya Kamesh Bhethanabhotla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间层次结构是否能提升LeWorldModel在长视野目标条件控制中的表现，提出了Hi-LeWM方法。结果显示，层次结构并非自动有效，性能提升依赖于高阶搜索与低层控制器的兼容性。通过约束搜索空间并合理设定子目标执行时机，可在中长期视野下显著超越原有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索时间层次结构能否提升预训练的低层次LeWM在长视野任务中的控制性能，并解决直接引入高层规划可能导致的性能下降问题。

Method: 提出Hi-LeWM，冻结预训练的低层次LeWM，增加基于潜在子目标的高层规划；通过实验分析不同视野长度下的高阶规划效果，并测试使用真实未来潜变量子目标及约束搜索策略的影响。

Result: 在短视野任务中，单步高层规划效果最佳；长视野任务中，原始高层动作空间与推理时搜索分布存在不匹配。使用训练轨迹编码的宏动作并约束搜索范围后，可显著提升性能，在PushT任务的中等和最长视野下分别提升+11.3和+14.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 时间抽象可以提升紧凑且冻结的LeWM模型性能，但前提是高层搜索必须与低层控制器保持兼容，合理的子目标生成和执行时机设计是关键。

Abstract: We investigate whether temporal hierarchy can improve LeWorldModel on long-horizon goal-conditioned control. We introduce Hi-LeWM, an extension that freezes the pretrained low-level LeWM and adds high-level planning over latent subgoals. We evaluate Hi-LeWM on PushT and Cube across increasing goal offsets. Hierarchy does not automatically improve performance: at short horizons, the best configuration uses a one-step high-level horizon, while longer horizons reveal a mismatch between the learned high-level action space and the inference-time search distribution. Experiments with true future latent subgoals show that the frozen low-level controller can execute well-aligned intermediate targets, indicating that high-level subgoal generation is the main bottleneck. Unconstrained search can select latent macro-actions that appear favorable under the learned model but produce poor control targets. Constraining search around macro-actions encoded from training trajectories, with appropriate subgoal execution timing, recovers useful hierarchical regimes, improving over flat LeWM by +11.3 percentage points at medium-range horizons and +14.7 percentage points at the longest PushT horizon. Overall, temporal abstraction can benefit compact frozen LeWM, but only when high-level search remains compatible with the low-level controller

</details>


### [161] [TrustVLA: Mechanism-Guided Inference-Time Defense Against Vision-Language-Action Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12571)
*Pinhan Fu,Xianda Guo,Xuetao Li,Wenke Huang,Ruilin Wang,Weiheng Zhao,Wei Sui,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中的视觉触发后门攻击问题，提出了TrustVLA防御方法，通过机制引导的推理时监控与局部修复，有效检测并恢复被污染的模型行为，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在用户无法审计的管道中部署，存在被植入隐蔽后门的风险；现有防御方法难以解释触发机制或在不重训练的情况下恢复正常行为。

Method: 基于识别出的“紧凑因果足迹”机制，提出TrustVLA：利用Dirichlet证据框架监控每层每令牌的认知不确定性，通过反事实机制评分下降定位异常区域，并采用局部图像修复恢复输入。

Result: 在OpenVLA/LIBERO和$π_{0.5}$迁移评估中，TrustVLA成功降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了对清洁任务的良好性能。

Conclusion: TrustVLA提供了一种无需重训练、基于机制理解的推理时防御方案，能够有效应对视觉触发的VLA后门攻击，增强了模型的可信赖性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are deployed through pipelines that end users cannot audit, and a poisoned VLA can behave normally on clean observations while a small visual trigger redirects a long-horizon robot policy before any failure becomes observable. Existing vision or language defenses rarely explain what a triggered VLA representation looks like or how to recover behavior without retraining. We study this gap through two independently proposed VLA attacks from groups with distinct injection strategies, BadVLA and INFUSE; the latter persists after downstream clean adaptation. Across the evaluated poisoned models, we identify a recurring internal mechanism: a \emph{compact causal footprint}, namely a small visual support that is attention-seeded, spatially compact, and \emph{causal} in a precise sense -- masking it returns a clean-calibrated evidence-evolution score to the normal operating region. This footprint motivates TrustVLA, a mechanism-guided inference-time defense that adapts the Dirichlet evidence framework from trusted classification to monitor per-token, per-layer epistemic uncertainty in VLA policies. With only a small clean calibration set, TrustVLA (i)~detects abnormal evidence evolution, (ii)~localizes the compact support by counterfactual mechanism-score drop, and (iii)~recovers the observation by localized inpainting. Across OpenVLA/LIBERO and $π_{0.5}$ transfer evaluations, TrustVLA reduces attack success while preserving clean-task performance, providing a retraining-free, mechanism-guided defense for visual-triggered VLA backdoors.

</details>


### [162] [Streamlining stereo differentiable rendering for marker-free real-time tracking of surgical robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12604)
*Yanghe Hao,Martin Huber,Christos Bergeles,Tom Vercauteren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于立体可微渲染的无标记手术机器人实时姿态跟踪方法，通过序列优化和CUDA并行加速，在1080p分辨率下实现30fps的跟踪速度，并在精度上优于现有基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于手术环境中存在遮挡，基于标记的机器人跟踪方法容易失效，因此需要一种鲁棒、实时的无标记跟踪方案以提高安全性、减少设置时间并支持多机器人协作。

Method: 扩展了无标记姿态估计框架roboreg，引入序列优化进行帧间姿态传播并结合运动自适应超参数调节，同时采用CUDA流并行和CUDA图加速分割与优化过程。

Result: 实现了1080p 30fps的实时跟踪（较原始roboreg提升至6倍速度），静态标定下精度达1.7 cm / 0.6 deg，动态参考对比平均3D误差为1.2 cm（遮挡下为1.53 cm）；相比FoundationPose动态估计提升11%（遮挡下提升63%），静态估计提升250%。

Conclusion: 立体可微渲染能够实现高分辨率、实时的无标记手术机器人姿态跟踪，性能媲美基于标记的方法，并显著超越基础模型基线。

Abstract: Purpose: Marker-based tracking of surgical robots is occlusion-prone in cluttered operating rooms. We evaluate stereo differentiable rendering for marker-free, real-time robot pose tracking, potentially improving safety, reducing setup time, and enabling multi-robot interaction. Methods: We extend the markerless pose estimation framework roboreg to online dynamic tracking via (i) sequential optimisation that propagates pose estimates across frames with motion-adaptive hyperparameter tuning, and (ii) CUDA stream parallelisation of segmentation and optimisation, combined with CUDA-graph accelerated segmentation. We evaluate on 38 unobstructed and 5 occluded displacement sequences with static start/end ground-truth calibrations and dynamic marker-based reference tracking. Results: We achieve real-time 1080p tracking at 30 fps (up from 14 fps for vanilla roboreg), matching the camera frame rate. Accuracy reaches 1.7 cm / 0.6 deg against static ground truth and 1.2 cm mean 3D error over 27,460 frames against the marker-based reference (1.53 cm over 1,242 occluded frames). Our method outperforms FoundationPose by 11% in dynamic estimation (63% under occlusion) and 250% in static estimation, with 6x faster inference. Conclusions: Stereo differentiable rendering enables real-time, high-resolution marker-free surgical robot tracking, on par with marker-based approaches and surpassing foundation-model baselines.

</details>


### [163] [Instance-Enriched Semantic Maps for Visual Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12630)
*Jiho Hong,Eunae Kang,Sanghyun Kim,Young-Sik Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于视觉语言导航（VLN）的实例增强型语义地图框架，通过2.5D实例级映射、基于大语言模型的鲁棒查询处理和高效存储表示，显著提升了复杂室内环境中的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN系统缺乏对物体实例的细粒度建模，且对多样化用户查询的适应能力弱，难以在复杂室内环境中实现可靠导航。

Method: 提出Instance-Enriched Semantic Maps框架：1）基于开放词汇全景分割构建2.5D实例级语义地图，保留垂直结构信息并捕获小物体；2）利用LLM进行目标选择，通过类型专用专家与评分层融合实现鲁棒查询处理；3）采用高效的语义存储表示，大幅降低存储开销。

Result: 所提2.5D表示在预测归一化AUC上比3D基线提升超27%；导航实验中，物体检索成功率提高17%以上，导航成功率提升23%以上，同时存储成本降低约96%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过增强实例级语义信息和优化查询处理机制，在保持低存储开销的同时显著提升了VLN在复杂环境中的有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Visual Language Navigation (VLN) aims to enable an embodied agent to navigate complex environments by following natural language instructions. Recent approaches build semantic spatial maps and leverage Large Language Models (LLMs) for reasoning and decision making. Despite these advances, existing systems lack instance-level object detail and robustness to diverse user queries, limiting reliable navigation in complex indoor environments. To address these limitations, we propose Instance-Enriched Semantic Maps, a unified framework with three key contributions: (1) Instance-level two-and-a-half-dimensional (2.5D) rich information mapping that constructs maps from color and depth observations via open-vocabulary panoptic segmentation, preserving vertical distinctions and capturing small objects, while storing diverse semantic attributes and natural language captions enriched with room-level context.
